| 2026-11-26 21:00 UTC | 2026-11-26 23:00 UTC | Fudbal - Liga Evrope: Juventus - Omonia | The second most prestigious club football competition in Europe is officially under the auspices of the European Football Federation UEFE from 1971. The unofficial predecessor of this competition was the Fair Cities Cup, which was played from 1955 to 1971. From that season, the competition was officially called the UEFA Cup and was under that name until 2009, when it was renamed the UEFA Europa League. Over the years, the format of the competition has changed several times, and since the 2004/2005 season. a group stage was introduced followed by eliminations. The trophy itself for the winner of this competition was designed in 1972 for the final of the Uefa Cup by the Italian artist Bertoni and is awarded every year to the winner who keeps it for a year. |
